[user message]
Work over \(\mathbb C\). Let \(\mathcal M_{25}\) be the Deligne–Mumford moduli stack of smooth, connected, projective curves of genus \(25\), and let \(\pi:\mathcal C_{25}\to\mathcal M_{25}\) be its universal curve. Write \(\mathrm{CH}^k(-;\mathbb Q)\) for the Chow group of codimension-\(k\) algebraic cycles with rational coefficients modulo rational equivalence. For each integer \(i\geq 1\), define
\[
\kappa_i=\pi_*\bigl(c_1(\omega_\pi)^{i+1}\bigr)\in\mathrm{CH}^i(\mathcal M_{25};\mathbb Q),
\]
where \(\omega_\pi\) is the relative dualizing line bundle, \(c_1\) is the first Chern class, and \(\pi_*\) is proper pushforward. Let \(R^*(\mathcal M_{25})\) be the graded \(\mathbb Q\)-subalgebra of \(\mathrm{CH}^*(\mathcal M_{25};\mathbb Q)\) generated by these classes, with grading by codimension. What is the exact value of \(\dim_{\mathbb Q}R^{12}(\mathcal M_{25})\)?

The answer is 44. This was proved recently, so it is not an open problem: your team's task is to prove it, completely, and to write Python that really computes or checks every step that can be checked. Printing what you believe is not evidence. Arriving at any other answer is not acceptable, and neither is conceding: when an approach fails, try another.

Proving 44 takes two halves: dim ≤ 44 (enough independent relations among the degree-12 kappa monomials) and dim ≥ 44 (enough independent linear functionals on degree-12 kappa polynomials that vanish on every true relation). Split the halves, and the pieces of each, between you on Slack.

Stop only when the team's proof is complete. Before you stop, post on Slack what you proved, and what is left.

```
from functools import lru_cache
from itertools import product
from math import factorial, comb
from collections import Counter

P=1000000007
def parts(n,m=None):
 if n==0: yield ();return
 if m is None:m=n
 for i in range(min(n,m),1-1,-1):
  for q in parts(n-i,i):yield (i,)+q

def df(n):
 ans=1
 for i in range(n,0,-2):ans=ans*i%P
 return ans
@lru_cache(None)
def pp(a):
 # set partition generating polynomial weighted Mobius and odd doublefactorial blocks
 if not a:return (1,)
 first=a[0]; c=Counter(a[1:]);ks=tuple(c); cs=tuple(c[k] for k in ks)
 out=[0]*(len(a)+1)
 for vv in product(*(range(v+1) for v in cs)):
  size=1+sum(vv); ss=first+sum(k*v for k,v in zip(ks,vv))
  mult=1
  for c0,v in zip(cs,vv):mult=mult*comb(c0,v)%P
  coeff=mult*factorial(size-1)*(-1)**(size-1)*pow(df(2*ss+1),P-2,P)%P
  rem=tuple(sorted((k for k,c0,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c0-v)),reverse=True))
  for j,z in enumerate(pp(rem)):out[j+1]=(out[j+1]+coeff*z)%P
 return tuple(out)
@lru_cache(None)
def value(a):
 g=25
 return sum(z*factorial(2*g-3+j) for j,z in enumerate(pp(a)))%P

a=list(parts(12));b=list(parts(11))
M=[[value(tuple(sorted(x+y,reverse=True))) for y in b] for x in a]
def rank(M):
 M=[r[:] for r in M];nr=len(M);nc=len(M[0]);r=0
 for c in range(nc):
  p=next((j for j in range(r,nr) if M[j][c]),None)
  if p is None:continue
  M[r],M[p]=M[p],M[r];v=pow(M[r][c],P-2,P);M[r]=[x*v%P for x in M[r]]
  for j in range(r+1,nr):
   v=M[j][c]
   if v:M[j]=[(x-v*y)%P for x,y in zip(M[j],M[r])]
  r+=1
  if r==nr:return r
 return r
print(len(a),len(b),rank(M),pp.cache_info())
import json
with open('workspace/agent4_pairing.json','w') as f:json.dump({'p':P,'rows':a,'cols':b,'matrix':M},f)
```

content:
```
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('workspace/agent4_pairing.py')
p.write_text('''"""Faber lambda_g lambda_{g-1} pairing, exact modulo a prime.
The common nonzero factor (2g-3)!!/(2g-2)! is omitted.
"""
from functools import lru_cache
from itertools import product
from math import factorial, comb
from collections import Counter
P=1000000007

def partitions(n,m=None):
    if n==0:
        yield (); return
    if m is None: m=n
    for i in range(min(n,m),0,-1):
        for q in partitions(n-i,i): yield (i,)+q

def odd_df(n):
    z=1
    for i in range(n,0,-2): z=z*i%P
    return z

@lru_cache(None)
def partition_polynomial(a):
    # Sum over set partitions, recording block count as power of z.
    if not a: return (1,)
    first=a[0]; c=Counter(a[1:]); ks=tuple(c); cs=tuple(c[k] for k in ks)
    out=[0]*(len(a)+1)
    for vv in product(*(range(v+1) for v in cs)):
        size=1+sum(vv); ss=first+sum(k*v for k,v in zip(ks,vv))
        mult=1
        for c0,v in zip(cs,vv): mult=mult*comb(c0,v)%P
        coeff=mult*(-1)**(size-1)*pow(odd_df(2*ss+1),P-2,P)%P
        rem=tuple(sorted((k for k,c0,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c0-v)),reverse=True))
        for j,z in enumerate(partition_polynomial(rem)): out[j+1]=(out[j+1]+coeff*z)%P
    return tuple(out)

@lru_cache(None)
def intersection(a,g=25):
    assert sum(a)==g-2
    return sum(z*factorial(2*g-3+j) for j,z in enumerate(partition_polynomial(a)))%P

def rank_certificate(M):
    A=[r[:] for r in M]; ids=list(range(len(A))); r=0; rows=[];cols=[];det=1
    for c in range(len(A[0])):
        j=next((j for j in range(r,len(A)) if A[j][c]),None)
        if j is None: continue
        A[r],A[j]=A[j],A[r];ids[r],ids[j]=ids[j],ids[r]
        rows.append(ids[r]);cols.append(c)
        v=A[r][c];det=det*v%P
        A[r]=[x*pow(v,P-2,P)%P for x in A[r]]
        for k in range(r+1,len(A)):
            v=A[k][c]
            if v: A[k]=[(x-v*y)%P for x,y in zip(A[k],A[r])]
        r+=1
        if r==len(A):break
    return r,rows,cols

def main():
    a=list(partitions(12));b=list(partitions(11))
    M=[[intersection(tuple(sorted(x+y,reverse=True))) for y in b] for x in a]
    rank, rows, cols=rank_certificate(M)
    assert rank==43
    print('Pairing shape',len(a),len(b),'rank mod',P,':',rank)
    print('43 by 43 nonzero minor rows:',rows,'columns:',cols)
    # Every supplied FZ relation must vanish on all 56 pairing columns.
    import json
    from fractions import Fraction
    fz=json.load(open('workspace/fz25_upper_data.json'))
    fb=[tuple(v) for v in fz['basis']]
    idx={v:i for i,v in enumerate(a)}
    for rel in fz['matrix']:
        rr=[]
        for x in rel:
            q=Fraction(str(x));rr.append(q.numerator*pow(q.denominator,P-2,P)%P)
        for j in range(len(b)):
            assert sum(x*M[idx[v]][j] for x,v in zip(rr,fb))%P==0
    print('All 59 FZ relation rows annihilate all pairing columns.')
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
import agent4_pairing
agent4_pairing.main()

```

```
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent4_pairing import *
from functools import lru_cache
from random import Random
@lru_cache(None)
def split(a):
 c=Counter(a);ks=tuple(c);cs=tuple(c[k] for k in ks);out=[]
 for vv in product(*(range(v+1) for v in cs)):
  x=tuple(sorted((k for k,v in zip(ks,vv) for _ in range(v)),reverse=True))
  y=tuple(sorted((k for k,c0,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c0-v)),reverse=True))
  z=1
  for c0,v in zip(cs,vv):z*=comb(c0,v)
  out.append((x,y,z))
 return out
@lru_cache(None)
def point(a,t,g):
 assert sum(a)+t==g-1
 out=0
 for x,y,z in split(a):
  s=t+sum(y)
  if s==0:continue
  k=s-1
  if k==0:v=(2*g-2)*intersection(x,g)
  else:v=intersection(tuple(sorted(x+(k,),reverse=True)),g)
  out+=z*v
 return out%P
basis=list(partitions(12));R=[];rng=Random(4);g=12;h=13
for run in range(300):
 degI=rng.randrange(12);degJ=rng.randrange(12-degI)
 I=rng.choice(list(partitions(degI)));J=rng.choice(list(partitions(degJ)))
 t=rng.randrange(12-degI-degJ);u=11-degI-degJ-t
 row=[]
 for p in basis:
  val=0
  for x,y,z in split(p):
   if sum(x)+degI+t!=g-1:continue
   val+=z*point(tuple(sorted(x+I,reverse=True)),t,g)*point(tuple(sorted(y+J,reverse=True)),u,h)
  row.append(val%P)
 R.append(row)
 rk=rank_certificate(R)[0]
 if run%10==0:print(run,rk,flush=True)
 if rk==77:break
print('final rank',rk)
```

```
import sys,time,json
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent4_pairing import partitions
from functools import lru_cache
from fractions import Fraction as Q
from itertools import product
from collections import Counter
from math import comb,factorial
from sympy import Matrix
@lru_cache(None)
def df(n):
 z=1
 for i in range(n,0,-2):z*=i
 return z
@lru_cache(None)
def pp(a):
 if not a:return (Q(1),)
 first=a[0];c=Counter(a[1:]);ks=tuple(c);cs=tuple(c[k] for k in ks);out=[Q(0)]*(len(a)+1)
 for vv in product(*(range(v+1) for v in cs)):
  size=1+sum(vv);s=first+sum(k*v for k,v in zip(ks,vv));mult=1
  for c0,v in zip(cs,vv):mult*=comb(c0,v)
  z=Q(mult*(-1)**(size-1),df(2*s+1))
  rem=tuple(sorted((k for k,c0,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c0-v)),reverse=True))
  for j,y in enumerate(pp(rem)):out[j+1]+=z*y
 return tuple(out)
@lru_cache(None)
def value(a):return sum(z*factorial(47+j) for j,z in enumerate(pp(a)))
a=list(partitions(12));b=list(partitions(11));t=time.time()
M=[[value(tuple(sorted(x+y,reverse=True))) for y in b] for x in a]

from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix
D=DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(Matrix(M)).to_field()
E,piv=D.rref()
print('exact rank',len(piv),'seconds',time.time()-t,flush=True)
assert len(piv)==43
json.dump({'basis12':a,'basis11':b,'matrix':[[str(v) for v in r] for r in M]},open('workspace/agent4_pairing_exact.json','w'))
```

```
import sys,time
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from agent4_pairing import *
from functools import lru_cache
from sage.all import GF,matrix,vector,block_matrix,zero_matrix
F=GF(P)
@lru_cache(None)
def splits(a):
 c=Counter(a);ks=tuple(c);cs=tuple(c[k] for k in ks);out=[]
 for vv in product(*(range(v+1) for v in cs)):
  x=tuple(sorted((k for k,v in zip(ks,vv) for _ in range(v)),reverse=True))
  y=tuple(sorted((k for k,c0,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c0-v)),reverse=True))
  z=1
  for c0,v in zip(cs,vv):z*=comb(c0,v)
  out.append((x,y,z))
 return out
@lru_cache(None)
def point(a,t,g):
 assert sum(a)+t==g-1
 out=0
 for x,y,z in splits(a):
  s=t+sum(y)
  if s==0:continue
  k=s-1
  if k==0:v=(2*g-2)*intersection(x,g)
  else:v=intersection(tuple(sorted(x+(k,),reverse=True)),g)
  out+=z*v
 return out%P

def mons(d):return [(a,d-sum(a)) for k in range(d+1) for a in partitions(k)]
def pair(x,y,g):return point(tuple(sorted(x[0]+y[0],reverse=True)),x[1]+y[1],g)

def factor(g):
 top=g-1;data={}
 for d in range(top+1):
  a=mons(d);b=mons(top-d)
  M=matrix(F,[[pair(x,y,g) for y in b] for x in a])
  rows=list(M.transpose().pivots());cols=list(M.pivots());
  data[d]={'basis':[a[i] for i in rows],'dual':[b[j] for j in cols],'pair':M.matrix_from_rows_and_columns(rows,cols)}
  print('factor',g,d,len(rows),flush=True)
 for d in range(top):
  A=data[d]['basis'];B=data[d+1]['dual']
  V=matrix(F,[[pair((x[0],x[1]+1),y,g) for y in B] for x in A])
  data[d]['psi']=(V*data[d+1]['pair'].inverse()).transpose()
 return data

t=time.time();A=factor(12);B=factor(13)
print('factor time',time.time()-t,flush=True)
# Degree11 source and degree12 target decomposed by A degree.
sizes11=[len(A[d]['basis'])*len(B[11-d]['basis']) for d in range(12)]
sizes12=[len(A[d]['basis'])*len(B[12-d]['basis']) for d in range(12)]
print('dimensions',sizes11,sum(sizes11),sizes12,sum(sizes12),flush=True)
blocks=[]
for d in range(12):
 row=[]
 for e in range(12):
  if d==e:
   v=matrix.identity(F,len(A[e]['basis'])).tensor_product(B[11-e]['psi'])
  elif d==e+1:
   v=A[e]['psi'].tensor_product(matrix.identity(F,len(B[11-e]['basis'])))
  else:v=zero_matrix(F,sizes12[d],sizes11[e])
  row.append(v)
 blocks.append(row)
M=block_matrix(F,blocks)
print('psi sum matrix',M.nrows(),M.ncols(),'rank',M.rank(),flush=True)
import pickle
pickle.dump((A,B,M),open('workspace/agent4_corrected.pkl','wb'))

```

```
from pathlib import Path
exec(Path('workspace/agent4_allcorrected.py').read_text().split('for g in range(1,13):')[0])
import json,pickle,os
from sage.all import bernoulli
allbasis=list(partitions(12))
Edata=json.load(open('workspace/fz25_exceptional.json'))
Ev=vector(F,[0]*77)
for a,v in zip(Edata['basis'],Edata['vector']):Ev[allbasis.index(tuple(a))]=F(int(v))

def boundary_monomial(p,x,y,g,h):
 I,t=x;J,u=y;ans=0
 for a,b,m in splits(p):
  if sum(a)+sum(I)+t!=g-1:continue
  if g==1:v=1
  else:v=point(tuple(sorted(a+I,reverse=True)),t,g)
  ans+=m*v*point(tuple(sorted(b+J,reverse=True)),u,h)
 return ans%P

def factorpair(A,g,d,e,psi):
 out=[]
 for a in A[d]['basis']:
  out.append([pair((a[0],a[1]+psi),b,g) for b in A[e]['basis']])
 return matrix(F,out)

def split_data(g):
 h=25-g;cache='workspace/agent4_boundarysplit_%02d.pkl'%g
 if os.path.exists(cache):return pickle.load(open(cache,'rb'))
 t0=time.time()
 if g==1:A={0:{'basis':[((),0)],'dual':[((),0)],'pair':matrix(F,[[1]])}}
 else:A=factor(g)
 B=factor(h)
 bases=[(x,y) for d in range(g) for x in A[d]['basis'] for y in B[11-d]['basis']]
 sizes=[len(A[d]['basis'])*len(B[11-d]['basis']) for d in range(g)]
 blocks=[]
 for d in range(g):
  rr=[]
  for e in range(g):
   if d+e==g-2:
    v=factorpair(A,g,d,e,1).tensor_product(factorpair(B,h,11-d,11-e,0))
   elif d+e==g-1:
    if g==1:Ap=matrix(F,[[1]])
    else:Ap=factorpair(A,g,d,e,0)
    v=Ap.tensor_product(factorpair(B,h,11-d,11-e,1))
   else:v=zero_matrix(F,sizes[d],sizes[e])
   rr.append(v)
  blocks.append(rr)
 S=block_matrix(F,blocks)
 print('built S',g,S.nrows(),time.time()-t0,flush=True)
 D=matrix(F,[[boundary_monomial(p,x,y,g,h) for p in allbasis] for x,y in bases])
 print('built D',g,D.nrows(),D.ncols(),time.time()-t0,flush=True)
 q=S.solve_right(D*Ev)
 answer=D.transpose()*q
 T=lambda k:F(abs(bernoulli(2*k)))/F(2**(2*k-1)*factorial(2*k))
 answer*=T(g)*T(h)
 pickle.dump((answer,S,D,bases),open(cache,'wb'))
 print('completed split',g,time.time()-t0,flush=True)
 return answer,S,D,bases
ans=vector(F,[0]*77)
for g in range(1,13):
 vv,*_=split_data(g);ans+=vv
json.dump({'p':P,'basis':allbasis,'boundary_pairing_exceptional':[int(v) for v in ans]},open('workspace/agent4_boundary_exceptional.json','w'))
print('boundary pairing functional computed',list(ans),flush=True)
Path('workspace/agent4_boundary_exceptional.py').write_text(Path('scratchpad.py').read_text().split("Path('workspace/agent4_boundary_exceptional.py')")[0])
```

```
"""Exact lambda_g times Chern-character Hodge integrals via Mumford GRR.
F(g, ds, ms) = int_{Mbar_g,n} lambda_g prod psi_i^ds_i prod ch_ms_j(E).
No unproved Gorenstein assumption enters.  All arithmetic is rational.
"""
from functools import lru_cache
from fractions import Fraction
P=1000000007
def Q(x,y=1):
    if isinstance(x,Fraction):return (x.numerator*pow(x.denominator,-1,P)*pow(y,-1,P))%P
    if hasattr(x,"q"):return int(x.p)*pow(int(x.q),-1,P)*pow(int(y),-1,P)%P
    return (int(x)%P)*pow(int(y)%P,-1,P)%P
from math import factorial, comb, prod
from collections import Counter
from itertools import product
import sympy as s

@lru_cache(None)
def b(g):
    if g==0:return Q(1)
    return Q((-1)**(g-1)*(2**(2*g-1)-1),2**(2*g-1))*Q(s.bernoulli(2*g),factorial(2*g))%P

@lru_cache(None)
def splits(t):
    items=list(Counter(t).items())
    def rec(i,a,c,m):
        if i==len(items):yield tuple(a),tuple(c),m;return
        v,n=items[i]
        for k in range(n+1):yield from rec(i+1,a+[v]*k,c+[v]*(n-k),m*comb(n,k))
    return tuple(rec(0,[],[],1))

def F(g,ds=(),ms=()):return _F(g,tuple(sorted(ds)),tuple(sorted(ms)))

@lru_cache(None)
def _F(g,ds,ms):
    n=len(ds)
    if 2*g-2+n<=0:return Q(0)
    if sum(ds)+sum(ms)!=2*g-3+n:return Q(0)
    if g==0:
        if ms:return Q(0)
        return Q(factorial(n-3),prod(factorial(x) for x in ds))
    if not ms:return b(g)*Q(factorial(sum(ds)),prod(factorial(x) for x in ds))%P
    assert all(m>0 and m%2 for m in ms)
    # String and dilaton relations: all Hodge classes pull back.
    if ds and ds[0]==0:
        restds=ds[1:];val=0
        for d,num in Counter(restds).items():
            if d:
                dd=list(restds);dd.remove(d);dd.append(d-1)
                val+=num*F(g,dd,ms)
        return val%P
    if 1 in ds:
        dd=list(ds);dd.remove(1)
        return (2*g-3+n)*F(g,dd,ms)%P
    m=ms[-1];rest=ms[:-1]
    value=F(g,ds+(m+1,),rest)
    for d,num in Counter(ds).items():
        dd=list(ds);dd.remove(d);dd.append(d+m)
        value-=num*F(g,dd,rest)
    boundary=Q(0)
    for ga in range(g+1):
      gb=g-ga
      for da,db,multd in splits(ds):
        if 2*ga-1+len(da)<=0 or 2*gb-1+len(db)<=0:continue
        for ma,mb,multm in splits(rest):
          j=2*ga-2+len(da)-sum(da)-sum(ma)
          if 0<=j<m:
            fa=F(ga,da+(j,),ma)
            if fa:boundary+=(-1)**j*multd*multm*fa*F(gb,db+(m-1-j,),mb)
    value+=boundary*pow(2,-1,P)
    return Q(s.bernoulli(m+1),factorial(m+1))*value%P

def odd_partitions(n,m=None):
    if n==0:yield ();return
    if m is None:m=n
    for i in range(min(n,m),0,-1):
        if i%2:
            for p in odd_partitions(n-i,i):yield (i,)+p

@lru_cache(None)
def lambda_expansion(k):
    # c_t(E)=exp(sum_{m odd}(m-1)! ch_m t^m).
    terms=[]
    for p in odd_partitions(k):
        coeff=Q(1)
        for m,cnt in Counter(p).items():coeff*=Q(factorial(m-1)**cnt,factorial(cnt))
        terms.append((p,coeff))
    return tuple(terms)

@lru_cache(None)
def kappa_expansion(a):
    # kappa products inverted from psi pushforwards: signed set partitions.
    if not a:return (((),1),)
    first=a[0];cc=Counter(a[1:]);ks=tuple(cc);cs=tuple(cc.values());out=Counter()
    for vv in product(*(range(c+1) for c in cs)):
        size=1+sum(vv);ss=first+sum(k*v for k,v in zip(ks,vv))
        mult=(-1)**(size-1)*prod(comb(c,v) for c,v in zip(cs,vv))
        rem=tuple(sorted(k for k,c,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c-v)))
        for p,z in kappa_expansion(rem):out[tuple(sorted(p+(ss,)))]+=mult*z
    return tuple((p,z) for p,z in out.items() if z)

def H(g,k,kappa=(),psi=()):
    # int lambda_g lambda_k prod kappa_i prod psi_j^dj.
    # kappa here is the log kappa on Mbar_g,len(psi).
    if sum(kappa)+sum(psi)+k!=2*g-3+len(psi):return Q(0)
    ans=Q(0)
    for p,c in lambda_expansion(k):
      for a,z in kappa_expansion(tuple(sorted(kappa))):
        ans+=c*z*F(g,tuple(psi)+tuple(i+1 for i in a),p)
    return ans%P

def validate():
    assert b(1)==Q(1,24) and b(2)==Q(7,5760)
    assert F(2,(),(1,))==Q(1,5760)
    assert F(3,(),(1,1,1))*pow(2,-1,P)%P==Q(1,1451520)
    assert H(3,2,(1,))==Q(1,120960)
    # Compare genus4 two kappa intersections with Faber ratio.
    x=H(4,3,(2,));y=H(4,3,(1,1))
    assert y==Q(32,3)*x%P,(x,y)
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent4_hodge_direct.py').write_text('''from functools import lru_cache
from math import factorial,prod
from collections import Counter
from sage.all import bernoulli
from agent1_hodge_mod import P,Q,b,splits

def J(g,k,ds=()):return _J(g,k,tuple(sorted(ds)))
@lru_cache(None)
def _J(g,k,ds):
 n=len(ds)
 if k<0 or k>g or 2*g-2+n<=0 or sum(ds)+k!=2*g-3+n:return 0
 if k==0:
  if g==0:return Q(factorial(n-3),prod(factorial(d) for d in ds))
  return b(g)*Q(factorial(sum(ds)),prod(factorial(d) for d in ds))%P
 if ds and ds[0]==0:
  rest=ds[1:];v=0
  for d,c in Counter(rest).items():
   if d:
    dd=list(rest);dd.remove(d);dd.append(d-1);v+=c*J(g,k,dd)
  return v%P
 if 1 in ds:
  dd=list(ds);dd.remove(1)
  return (2*g-3+n)*J(g,k,dd)%P
 out=0
 for m in range(k if k%2 else k-1,0,-2):
  kk=k-m
  v=J(g,kk,ds+(m+1,))
  for d,c in Counter(ds).items():
   dd=list(ds);dd.remove(d);dd.append(d+m)
   v-=c*J(g,kk,dd)
  boundary=0
  for ga in range(g+1):
   gb=g-ga
   for da,db,cm in splits(ds):
    na=len(da);nb=len(db)
    if 2*ga-1+na<=0 or 2*gb-1+nb<=0:continue
    for ia in range(max(0,kk-gb),min(ga,kk)+1):
     ib=kk-ia;r=2*ga-2+na-sum(da)-ia
     if not 0<=r<m:continue
     x=J(ga,ia,da+(r,))
     if x:boundary+=(-1)**r*cm*x*J(gb,ib,db+(m-1-r,))
  v+=boundary*pow(2,-1,P)
  out+=Q(bernoulli(m+1),m+1)*v
 return out*pow(k,-1,P)%P
''')
```

```
def topconst(g):return Q(abs(bernoulli(2*g)),2**(2*g-1)*factorial(2*g))

def J(g,k,ds=()):return _J(g,k,tuple(sorted(ds)))
@lru_cache(None)
def _J(g,k,ds):
 n=len(ds)
 if k<0 or k>g or 2*g-2+n<=0 or sum(ds)+k!=2*g-3+n:return 0
 if g>0 and k==g:return 0
 if k==0:
  if g==0:return Q(factorial(n-3),prod(factorial(d) for d in ds))
  return b(g)*Q(factorial(sum(ds)),prod(factorial(d) for d in ds))%P
 if ds and ds[0]==0:
  rest=ds[1:];v=0
  for d,c in Counter(rest).items():
   if d:
    dd=list(rest);dd.remove(d);dd.append(d-1);v+=c*J(g,k,dd)
  return v%P
 if 1 in ds:
  dd=list(ds);dd.remove(1)
  return (2*g-3+n)*J(g,k,dd)%P
 if g>1 and k==g-1:
  return topconst(g)*Q(factorial(2*g-3+n),prod(odd_df(2*d-1) for d in ds))%P
 out=0
 for m in range(k if k%2 else k-1,0,-2):
  kk=k-m
  v=J(g,kk,ds+(m+1,))
  for d,c in Counter(ds).items():
   dd=list(ds);dd.remove(d);dd.append(d+m)
   v-=c*J(g,kk,dd)
  boundary=0
  for ga in range(g+1):
   gb=g-ga
   for da,db,cm in splits(ds):
    na=len(da);nb=len(db)
    if 2*ga-1+na<=0 or 2*gb-1+nb<=0:continue
    for ia in range(max(0,kk-gb),min(ga,kk)+1):
     ib=kk-ia;r=2*ga-2+na-sum(da)-ia
     if not 0<=r<m:continue
     x=J(ga,ia,da+(r,))
     if x:boundary+=(-1)**r*cm*x*J(gb,ib,db+(m-1-r,))
  v+=boundary*pow(2,-1,P)
  out+=BERN[m]*v
 return out*pow(k,-1,P)%P
```

```
import sys,json,pickle,time,os
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
import agent2_pointed_fz as T
from functools import lru_cache
from collections import Counter
from itertools import product
from math import comb
from sage.all import matrix,QQ,vector
P=T.P;start=time.time()

def qrows(h,d):
 idx={x:i for i,x in enumerate(T.basis(d))}
 for r in range(1,d+1):
  for a in (0,1):
   for sz in range(min(12,3*r-h-a-1)+1):
    if (3*r-h-1-a-sz)%2:continue
    for sig in T.F.partitions(sz):
     if any(s%3==2 for s in sig):continue
     rr={}
     for t in range(r+1):
      for q,c in T.unrel(h,r-t,sig).items():rr[(q,t)]=c*(T.F.A if a==0 else T.F.B)[t]
     for ex,ps in T.basis(d-r):
      row=[QQ(0)]*len(idx)
      for (kp,t),c in rr.items():row[idx[(tuple(sorted(kp+ex,reverse=True)),t+ps)]]=QQ(str(c))
      yield row
@lru_cache(None)
def factor(h,d):
 path='workspace/agent2_exactfactor_%02d_%02d.pkl'%(h,d)
 if False and os.path.exists(path):return pickle.load(open(path,'rb'))
 n=len(T.basis(d))
 if d>h-1:out={'basis':T.basis(d),'free':[],'red':[[] for _ in range(n)],'dim':0}
 else:
  piv={};selected=[]
  for qr in qrows(h,d):
   row=[int(x.numerator()%P)*pow(int(x.denominator()%P),-1,P)%P for x in qr]
   for j in range(n):
    v=row[j]
    if not v:continue
    if j in piv:row=[(x-v*y)%P for x,y in zip(row,piv[j])]
    else:
     inv=pow(v,-1,P);piv[j]=[x*inv%P for x in row];selected.append(qr);break
  E=matrix(QQ,selected,ncols=n).echelon_form() if selected else matrix(QQ,0,n)
  pc=E.pivots();assert len(pc)==len(piv)
  free=[j for j in range(n) if j not in pc];red=[]
  for j in range(n):
   if j in pc:
    i=pc.index(j);red.append([-E[i,k] for k in free])
   else:red.append([QQ(int(j==k)) for k in free])
  out={'basis':T.basis(d),'free':free,'red':red,'dim':len(free)}
 pickle.dump(out,open(path,'wb'));return out
@lru_cache(None)
def psi(h,d):
 a=factor(h,d);b=factor(h,d+1);idx={v:i for i,v in enumerate(b['basis'])}
 return [b['red'][idx[(a['basis'][j][0],a['basis'][j][1]+1)]] for j in a['free']]
@lru_cache(None)
def splits(a):
 c=Counter(a);ks=tuple(c);cs=tuple(c[k] for k in ks);out=[]
 for vv in product(*(range(v+1) for v in cs)):
  x=tuple(k for k,v in zip(ks,vv) for _ in range(v));y=tuple(k for k,c0,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c0-v));z=1
  for c0,v in zip(cs,vv):z*=comb(c0,v)
  out.append((x,y,z))
 return out

def layout(a,b,d):
 keys=[]
 for i in range(d+1):
  for x in range(factor(a,i)['dim']):
   for y in range(factor(b,d-i)['dim']):keys.append((i,x,y))
 return keys,{k:i for i,k in enumerate(keys)}
def run(a):
 b=25-a;dom,di=layout(a,b,11);cod,ci=layout(a,b,12)
 print('factors',a,len(dom),len(cod),time.time()-start,flush=True)
 M=matrix(QQ,len(cod),len(dom),sparse=False)
 for j,(d,x,y) in enumerate(dom):
  for i,c in enumerate(psi(a,d)[x]):
   if c:M[ci[(d+1,i,y)],j]+=c
  for i,c in enumerate(psi(b,11-d)[y]):
   if c:M[ci[(d,x,i)],j]+=c
 V=vector(QQ,len(cod));p=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_Pgood.json'))
 for part,coeff in p['terms']:
  c0=QQ(coeff)
  for x,y,mult in splits(tuple(part)):
   d=sum(x)
   if d>a-1 or 12-d>b-1:continue
   fa=factor(a,d);fb=factor(b,12-d)
   ra=fa['red'][fa['basis'].index((x,0))];rb=fb['red'][fb['basis'].index((y,0))]
   for i,c in enumerate(ra):
    if c:
     for j,e in enumerate(rb):
      if e:V[ci[(d,i,j)]]+=c0*mult*c*e
 print('solving',a,time.time()-start,flush=True)
 sol=M.solve_right(V);assert M*sol==V
 pickle.dump({'M':M,'target':V,'solution':sol,'domain':dom,'codomain':cod},open('workspace/agent2_exact_divisibility_%02d.pkl'%a,'wb'))
 print('EXACT SUCCESS',a,time.time()-start,flush=True)
 return {'split':a,'shape':M.dimensions(),'exact_solution':True}
if __name__=='__main__':
 out=[]
 for a in range(1,13):
  out.append(run(a));json.dump(out,open('workspace/agent2_exact_divisibility.json','w'))
```

```
 I,t=x;J,u=y;out=0
```

```
from functools import lru_cache
from collections import Counter
from itertools import product
from math import comb,factorial,prod
from agent1_hodge_mod import P,Q,b
from sympy import bernoulli
BERN={m:Q(bernoulli(m+1),m+1) for m in range(1,50,2)}
INV={k:pow(k,-1,P) for k in range(1,51)}
HALF=INV[2]
FACT=[factorial(i)%P for i in range(110)]
IFACT=[pow(x,-1,P) for x in FACT]
@lru_cache(None)
def top(g):return Q(abs(bernoulli(2*g)),2**(2*g-1)*factorial(2*g))
@lru_cache(None)
def odf(n):return prod(range(n,0,-2))%P
@lru_cache(maxsize=128)
def splits(a):
 cc=Counter(a);ks=tuple(cc);cs=tuple(cc.values());out=[]
 for vv in product(*(range(c+1) for c in cs)):
  aa=tuple(x for x,c in zip(ks,vv) for _ in range(c));bb=tuple(x for x,c,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c-v))
  mult=prod(comb(c,v) for c,v in zip(cs,vv));out.append((aa,bb,mult,sum(aa)))
 return out

def K(g,k,a):
 if not 0<=k<g or sum(a)!=2*g-3-k:return 0
 return _K(g,k,tuple(sorted(a)))

@lru_cache(None)
def node(g,k,a,r):
 n=len(a)
 if k==0:return b(g)*FACT[sum(a)+n+r]*prod(IFACT[x+1] for x in a)*IFACT[r]%P
 if r==0:
  out=0
  for x,c in Counter(a).items():
   aa=list(a);aa.remove(x)
   if x==1:out+=c*(2*g-3+n)*K(g,k,tuple(aa))
   else:aa.append(x-1);out+=c*K(g,k,tuple(aa))
  return out%P
 if r==1:return (2*g-2+n)*K(g,k,a)%P
 return K(g,k,a+(r-1,))

@lru_cache(None)
def _K(g,k,a):
 n=len(a);A=sum(a)
 if k==0:return b(g)*FACT[A+n]*prod(IFACT[x+1] for x in a)%P
 if k==g-1:return top(g)*FACT[2*g-3+n]*pow(prod(odf(2*x+1) for x in a)%P,-1,P)%P
 out=0
 ct=Counter(a)
 for m in range(k if k%2 else k-1,0,-2):
  kk=k-m
  v=K(g,kk,a+(m,))
  for x,c in ct.items():
   aa=list(a);aa.remove(x);aa.append(x+m);v-=c*K(g,kk,tuple(aa))
  boundary=0
  for aa,bb,mult,Aa in splits(a):
   Ab=A-Aa
   glo=max(1,(Aa+3)//2,g-Ab-m)
   ghi=min(g-1,Aa+m,g-(Ab+3)//2)
   for ga in range(glo,ghi+1):
    gb=g-ga

     ib=kk-ia;r=2*ga-2-Aa-ia
     x=node(ga,ia,aa,r)
     if x:boundary+=(-1)**r*mult*x*node(gb,ib,bb,m-1-r)
  out+=BERN[m]*(v+boundary*HALF)
 return out*INV[k]%P

def J(g,k,ds):
 ds=tuple(ds)
 if k<0 or k>g or sum(ds)+k!=2*g-3+len(ds):return 0
 if g==0:return Q(factorial(len(ds)-3),prod(factorial(d) for d in ds)) if k==0 else 0
 if k==g:return 0
 if k==0:return b(g)*FACT[sum(ds)]*prod(IFACT[d] for d in ds)%P
 if 0 in ds:

  for d,c in Counter(rest).items():
   if d:
    aa=rest.copy();aa.remove(d);aa.append(d-1);out+=c*J(g,k,aa)
  return out%P
 if 1 in ds:
  aa=list(ds);aa.remove(1);return (2*g-3+len(ds))*J(g,k,aa)%P
 return K(g,k,tuple(d-1 for d in ds))
```

```
else:CACHES={}

    with open(CACHEFILE+'.tmp','wb') as _f:pickle.dump(CACHES,_f,protocol=5)
    os.replace(CACHEFILE+'.tmp',CACHEFILE);_LASTSAVE=time.time()
def memo(f):

    def wrap(*a):
        if a in cache:return cache[a]
        v=f(*a);cache[a]=v
        if len(cache)%10000==0 and time.time()-_LASTSAVE>120:save()
        return v
    wrap.cache_info=lambda:len(cache)
```

```
from sage.all import GF,vector,bernoulli
from math import factorial
p=1000000007;F=GF(p)
x=json.load(open('workspace/agent3_reduced_1.json'));Pv=vector(F,x['P']);Ev=vector(F,x['E'])
T=lambda k:F(abs(bernoulli(2*k)))/F(2**(2*k-1)*factorial(2*k))
ans=F(0);vals=[]
for g in range(1,13):
 t=time.time();_,S,D,_=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent4_boundarysplit_%02d.pkl'%g,'rb'))
 v=(D*Pv).dot_product(S.solve_right(D*Ev))*T(g)*T(25-g)
 vals.append(int(v));ans+=v
 print(g,int(v),time.time()-t,flush=True)
print('REDUCED_1 BOUNDARYSCALAR',int(ans),flush=True)
json.dump({'p':p,'scalar':int(ans),'contributions':vals},open('workspace/agent4_boundary_reduced1_scalar.json','w'))
```

```
from functools import lru_cache
from itertools import product
from math import factorial, comb, gcd, lcm
from collections import Counter
import gmpy2 as gp
import json,time
import sympy as sp
T=time.time()
Q=gp.mpq
@lru_cache(None)
def odd_df(n):
 z=1
 for i in range(n,0,-2):z*=i
 return z
@lru_cache(None)
def poly(a):
 if not a:return (Q(1),)
 first=a[0];c=Counter(a[1:]);ks=tuple(c);cs=tuple(c[k] for k in ks)
 out=[Q(0)]*(len(a)+1)
 for vv in product(*(range(v+1) for v in cs)):
  size=1+sum(vv);ss=first+sum(k*v for k,v in zip(ks,vv));mult=1
  for c0,v in zip(cs,vv):mult*=comb(c0,v)
  coeff=Q(mult*(-1)**(size-1),odd_df(2*ss+1))
  rem=tuple(sorted((k for k,c0,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c0-v)),reverse=True))
  for j,z in enumerate(poly(rem)):out[j+1]+=coeff*z
 return tuple(out)
@lru_cache(None)
def integral(a):return sum(z*factorial(47+j) for j,z in enumerate(poly(a)))
d=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_pairing.json'));a=[tuple(v) for v in d['rows']][33:];b=[tuple(v) for v in d['cols']]
M=[]
for i,x in enumerate(a):
 M.append([integral(tuple(sorted(x+y,reverse=True))) for y in b])
 if i%5==0:print(i, time.time()-T,poly.cache_info(),flush=True)
mat=sp.Matrix([[sp.Rational(str(c)) for c in row] for row in M]);print('created',time.time()-T,flush=True)
dm=sp.polys.matrices.DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(mat.T).convert_to(sp.QQ)
ns=dm.nullspace().to_Matrix();print('nullspace',ns.shape,time.time()-T,flush=True)
assert ns.rows==1
v=list(ns.row(0));den=lcm(*(int(c.q) for c in v));v=[int(c*den) for c in v];gc=gcd(*v);v=[c//gc for c in v]
assert mat.T*sp.Matrix(v)==sp.zeros(56,1)
print('digits', [len(str(c)) for c in v],flush=True)

"""Recompute the upper bound and every exact boundary-support certificate.
Run from the directory containing workspace/: python workspace/agent2_verify_support.py
Requires Python, SymPy, and SageMath (imported in the same interpreter).

Geometric inputs, not numerical assumptions:
(1) Faber--Zagier/Pixton relations on M_g and M_{g,1};
(2) R^d(M_{g,1})=0 for d>g-1 (pointed socle vanishing).
The pointed variables are logarithmic kappa, so kappa_0=2g-1.

For each a+b=25 this code constructs quotient reductions using actual pointed
FZ relations, then solves OVER Q
 P(kappa^a+kappa^b) = (psi_a+psi_b) Q_{a,b} mod (I_a+I_b).
It checks the exact equality, never inferring consistency from modular rank.
Multiplication by lambda_a lambda_{a-1} lambda_b lambda_{b-1} turns these
open pointed relations into equalities on the compactifications, since that
Hodge product restricts to zero on every boundary of Mbar_{h,1}.
"""
import json, runpy, sys, time
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('.')
assert (root/'workspace').is_dir(), 'Run from the directory containing workspace/'
sys.path.insert(0, 'workspace')
start=time.time()
for name in ('fz25_upper.py','agent2_candidate.py','agent2_exact_divisibility.py'):
    print('RECOMPUTING',name,flush=True)
    runpy.run_path(str(root/'workspace'/name),run_name='__main__')
checks=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_exact_divisibility.json'))
assert len(checks)==12 and {v['split'] for v in checks}==set(range(1,13))
assert all(v['exact_solution'] for v in checks)
print('VERIFIED: 33 independent true degree12 relations, candidate P, and all12 exact support equations.',flush=True)
print('Elapsed seconds',time.time()-start)
```

```
from pathlib import Path
Path('workspace/agent4_verify_lower.py').write_text('''"""Reproducible lower-bound verifier (Python + Sage + SymPy).
Run after agent2_verify_support.py, from the directory containing workspace/.
  python workspace/agent4_verify_lower.py
  python workspace/agent4_verify_lower.py --recompute-roots
The second command recomputes every genus25 Hodge root from Mumford-GRR /
Newton recursion; it can take hours from a cold cache. No FZ-completeness or
Gorenstein hypothesis is used. The default checks the already computed roots,
recomputes the 1574-term aggregation and all twelve boundary integrals from
the EXACT rational supported-Q certificates.
"""
import sys,json,pickle,time,glob,runpy
from pathlib import Path
from fractions import Fraction
from functools import lru_cache
from collections import Counter
from itertools import product
from math import comb,factorial
sys.path.insert(0,'workspace')
from sage.all import GF,bernoulli
from agent4_pairing import P,partitions,intersection
F=GF(P)

@lru_cache(None)
def splits(a):
 c=Counter(a);ks=tuple(c);cs=tuple(c[k] for k in ks);out=[]
 for vv in product(*(range(v+1) for v in cs)):
  x=tuple(sorted((k for k,v in zip(ks,vv) for _ in range(v)),reverse=True))
  y=tuple(sorted((k for k,c0,v in zip(ks,cs,vv) for _ in range(c0-v)),reverse=True))
  z=1
  for c0,v in zip(cs,vv):z*=comb(c0,v)
  out.append((x,y,z))
 return out

@lru_cache(None)
def point(a,t,g):
 # Raw integral on Mbar_g,1 with logarithmic kappa. Multiply by T_g below.
 assert sum(a)+t==g-1
 out=0
 for x,y,z in splits(a):
  s=t+sum(y)
  if s==0:continue
  k=s-1
  if k==0:v=(2*g-2)*intersection(x,g)
  else:v=intersection(tuple(sorted(x+(k,),reverse=True)),g)
  out+=z*v
 return out%P

@lru_cache(None)
def factor(h,d):
 return pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_exactfactor_%02d_%02d.pkl'%(h,d),'rb'))

def boundary(E):
 def value(g,h,x,y):
  I,t=x;J,u=y;out=0
  for p,c in E:
   v=0
   for a,b,z in splits(p):
    if sum(a)+sum(I)+t!=g-1:continue
    va=1 if g==1 else point(tuple(sorted(a+I,reverse=True)),t,g)
    v+=z*va*point(tuple(sorted(b+J,reverse=True)),u,h)
   out+=c*(v%P)
  return out%P
 T=lambda k:F(abs(bernoulli(2*k)))/F(2**(2*k-1)*factorial(2*k))
 total=F(0);vals=[]
 for g in range(1,13):
  h=25-g;cert=pickle.load(open('workspace/agent2_exact_divisibility_%02d.pkl'%g,'rb'))
  assert cert['M']*cert['solution']==cert['target']
  ans=F(0)
  for v,(d,x,y) in zip(cert['solution'],cert['domain']):
   if not v:continue
   a=factor(g,d);b=factor(h,11-d)
   xx=a['basis'][a['free'][x]];yy=b['basis'][b['free'][y]]
   ans+=F(v)*value(g,h,xx,yy)
  ans*=T(g)*T(h);total+=ans;vals.append(int(ans))
 return int(total),vals

def roots():
 values={}
 files=['agent4_J0_500.json','agent1_J500_1000.json',
 'agent1_Joriginal500_1000.json','agent3_J1000_1250.json',
 'agent2_J1250_1575.json','agent4_J1400_1575.json',
 'agent1_J1200_1250.json','agent1_J1450_1500.json','agent3_J1500_1575.json']
 for name in files:
  path=Path('workspace')/name
  if not path.exists():continue
  for i,v in json.load(open(path)).items():
   i=int(i);v=int(v)
   if i in values:assert values[i]==v,(name,i)
   values[i]=v
 return values

def main():
 import sympy as sp
 from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix
 # Recompute all standard intersections rationally and exhibit the exceptional class.
 runpy.run_path('workspace/agent4_pairing_exact.py',run_name='__main__')
 data=json.load(open('workspace/agent4_pairing_exact.json'))
 basis=[tuple(v) for v in data['basis12']]
 M=sp.Matrix([[sp.Rational(v) for v in row] for row in data['matrix']])
 assert DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(M).rank()==43
 ed=json.load(open('workspace/fz25_exceptional.json'))
 E=[(tuple(a),int(c)) for a,c in zip(ed['basis'],ed['vector'])]
 e=sp.zeros(77,1)
 for a,c in E:e[basis.index(a)]=c
 assert M.T*e==sp.zeros(56,1)
 fz=json.load(open('workspace/fz25_upper_data.json'))
 assert fz['basis']==data['basis12']
 R=sp.Matrix([[sp.Rational(c) for c in row] for row in fz['matrix']])
 assert R*M==sp.zeros(59,56)
 assert DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(R.col_join(e.T)).rank()==34
 print('Standard rank43 and exceptional non-FZ class checked exactly.',flush=True)
 # Recompute psi-pushforward expansion of Pgood times E; never trust aggregate data.
 import agent1_hodge_mod as H
 pd=json.load(open('workspace/agent2_Pgood.json'))
 W=Counter()
 for a,c in pd['terms']:
  cc=H.Q(Fraction(c))
  for b,d in E:
   for p,z in H.kappa_expansion(tuple(sorted(tuple(a)+b))):
    W[p]=(W[p]+cc*d*z)%P
 W={a:c for a,c in W.items() if c}
 assert len(W)==1574
 ps=list(partitions(24));idx={a:i for i,a in enumerate(ps)}
 values=roots()
 if '--recompute-roots' in sys.argv:
  import agent1_Kpersist as K
  K.CACHE_FILE='workspace/lower_verifier_Kcache.pkl'
  for j,a in enumerate(W):
   i=idx[tuple(sorted(a,reverse=True))]
   v=int(K.J(25,23,tuple(x+1 for x in a)))
   if i in values:assert values[i]==v,(i,v,values[i])
   values[i]=v
   if j%20==0:K.save();print('Recomputed Hodge roots',j,len(W),flush=True)
  K.save()
 missing=[idx[tuple(sorted(a,reverse=True))] for a in W if idx[tuple(sorted(a,reverse=True))] not in values]
 assert not missing,missing
 bulk=sum(c*values[idx[tuple(sorted(a,reverse=True))]] for a,c in W.items())%P
 bnd,contributions=boundary([(a,c%P) for a,c in E])
 total=(bulk+bnd)%P
 print('Bulk',bulk,'boundary',bnd,'corrected',total,flush=True)
 assert bnd==296879567
 assert bulk==799683810
 assert total==96563370 and total!=0
 print('VERIFIED: 43 standard functionals plus one independent supported Hodge functional give dimension at least44.')
if __name__=='__main__':main()
''')
```
